# Passenger Agent OS — Progress

Append-only worklog. Every agent adds an entry when it finishes a task — what shipped, what it learned, what it left behind. **Newest first — insert your entry at the top, never append at end-of-file** (L-003). `BOARD.md` holds current state; this holds history.

Entry format:

```
### YYYY-MM-DD — <agent> — <task id> <short title>
- **Did:** what actually changed (files, migrations, issue IDs)
- **Evidence:** build/test output, screenshots, review verdicts
- **Left behind:** follow-ups, known gaps, anything the next agent needs
```

---

### 2026-08-07 — product — T-051/`PAS-39` fixture-gap acceptance: ACCEPT (both P0s re-derived from source, and re-checked against the *newer* 44-hood polygon file QA never saw)

**Verdict: ACCEPT.** Both fixture gaps are closed. Re-derived independently from source at `passenger-code eecccd5` (confirmed an ancestor of `HEAD`; both fixture files untouched in the dirty tree, whose modifications are all Hoods/Search files from another session), not inherited from `qa`'s PASS (`passenger-brain 5f9425e`).

**P0 1 — bucket +5 rows visually distinct.** The +5 bucket is `start_offset_minutes: 300`, `duration_minutes: 60` (`EventSeed.swift:58` — `anchorHour + startOffsetMinutes × 60`), which is exactly `seed-0003`–`seed-0016`, 14 events. All 14 carry distinct names, distinct `(lat, lng)` pairs, distinct ranks, 9 distinct categories across 8 distinct hoods. Zero duplicate coordinates and zero duplicate names anywhere in the file, not just in that bucket. No name matches a stem-plus-index pattern. Nearest pair is `seed-0004`/`seed-0010` at ~0.00049° ≈ **55m** — separate pins at any usable zoom, against the 14-on-one-point the ticket was filed for.

**Beyond `qa`'s scope — the drift check acceptance owes.** `hoods-tel-aviv.json` was **regenerated after `eecccd5`** (`passenger-code 036d7d1` 24→44 hoods, then `11cb097` aliases), so every containment claim in this ticket's history was made against a polygon file that no longer exists. Re-ran ray-cast containment against the **current** 44-hood file (polygon vertices are `[lng, lat]`, GeoJSON order — an easy inversion that reports 100% failure if you get it backwards): **all 18 events and all 9 places land inside their claimed hood.** No drift. Also re-checked TRD §3.4 clause 8: zero event-name collisions against place names or hood names.

**P0 2 — closed + flagged demonstrated.** `kerem-carmel-spice-corner` is the sole place with both `permanently_closed: true` and `is_tourist_trap: true`. The file also retains a closed-only control (`neve-nachum-gutman-museum`) and a flagged-only control (`florentin-anna-loulou-bar`), so all three combinations render — the independence claim is now demonstrable in three directions, not one. Confirmed the two cues are genuinely independent surfaces at source: closed badge gated on `place.permanentlyClosed` in `PlacesListRow.swift:37`, "Tourist-heavy spot" gated on `place.isTouristTrap == true` in `PlaceDetailModal.swift:102`. No shared condition, no shared copy, and the tourist-heavy line never appears on a Places row (`places-been-saved` req 4 bullet 3).

**DoD "regenerated via the normal fixture pipeline, not hand-edited" — satisfied, and the phrasing is a trap worth naming.** There is no pipeline to bypass. `places-tel-aviv.json`'s own `_note` states it is hand-authored and that `export_places.py` "does not exist until T-042 step B3 (Build Phase 2)"; `events-tel-aviv-seed.json` has no generator anywhere in either repo (the only scripts are `build_hoods.py` / `export_hoods_geojson.py`, hood-only). Hand-authoring **is** the normal Phase-1 pipeline for both files. Verified by search, not taken from the board's prior claim.

**Spec defect fixed in this same commit (L-009).** The plausibility rule lived only in `live-events-overlay.md`'s Technical-design prose and TRD §3.4 clause 9 — never as a checkbox under a P0 — which is precisely why 14 identically-named stacked events passed every gate the first time. Worse, the prose still asserted the violation in the present tense after the fix shipped. Both fixed: a falsifiable bullet added under req 1, phrased as the rendered consequence a user sees rather than the stored value, plus a Decisions-log row. Added a matching closure row to `tourist-trap-flag.md` naming the standing data need — T-042's `export_places.py` overwrites `places-tel-aviv.json` wholesale in Build Phase 2 and must preserve all three closed/flagged combinations or both bullets go undemonstrable again.

**No build run, deliberately (L-040).** Preflight: no `HALT` in force (the 2026-08-04 disk halt is struck/lifted), `df -h /` 33Gi avail / 27% used — clear. But `uptime` read load averages **16.06 / 129.31 / 110.66** and `ps` shows another session's simulator live (12 `MTLCompilerService`, `SimRenderServer`, iOS 26.5 runtime). Adding a concurrent `xcodebuild` under that is the exact contention L-040 and the 2026-08-04 four-way dispatch note warn against. Nothing in this ticket needs one: both P0s are pure fixture-data claims, fully re-derivable at source, and `qa` already produced the live on-device evidence (37/37, closed badge + "Tourist-heavy spot" rendering confirmed via XCUITest) at `passenger-brain 5f9425e`. Recording this as a judgment call, not a skipped step — if a later gate wants a build attributable to this commit alone, it should be run under normal load.

**PRD `Status:` lines left alone, on purpose (L-006).** T-051 is a fixture-gap task, not a feature ship. `live-events-overlay.md` stays "Accepted (Build Phase 1) 2026-08-03 — reqs 1–5 only" and `tourist-trap-flag.md` stays Accepted 2026-08-03; neither statement is made stale by this fix.

Board row and Linear `PAS-39` are `chief-of-staff`'s to write — not touched here. Routing this asks for: T-051 → `aviran-review`.

### 2026-08-07 — product — T-048/`PAS-35` Local threshold acceptance: ACCEPT (value 2 stands, and is forced rather than chosen)

- **Verdict: ACCEPT.** The DoD ("value confirmed or changed at the single declaration site") is met. Task moves to `aviran-review`, not straight to `done`. `chief-of-staff` owns writing this to `PAS-35` and `BOARD.md`; I touched neither.
- **Re-derived from source, not from `qa`'s report.** `grep -rn threshold --include=*.swift Passenger` → one declaration, `Passenger/Passport/LocalStatus.swift:27` (`static let threshold = 2`); the four other hits (`PassportLabels`, `PassportProgressList` ×2, `LocalStatus.isLocal`) all *read* `LocalStatus.threshold` — no second literal compared against a Been count anywhere, which is req 4's last bullet and TRD §9 row 4(d). The doc comment's stale **[ASSUMPTION]** / "Aviran's to set" text is gone and now states decision #45's reasoning — that was D6's one named code follow-up, and it is done.
- **Fixture re-derived independently** (`python3` over the three shipped JSONs, field names read from the files rather than assumed — `hood_id`/`place_id`/`kind`, not the camelCase the report implies): 44 Hoods, exactly `florentin`/`kerem-hateimanim`/`neve-tzedek` designated; 9 places, 3/3/3 in exactly those Hoods; 2 `been` visits, both attributed to `kerem-hateimanim`. So `kerem-hateimanim` → Local, the other two → 0, overall → false. Genuine variance, as `qa` found.
- **The product call, which the DoD asked for and is the substance of this pass: 2 is forced, not chosen.** Decision #45 bounded the value to [2, 3] — floor 2 (at 1 Local is true on first contact), ceiling "the smallest curated place count across designated Hoods, 3 today" — then rejected 3 for equalling 100% catalogue completion with zero margin. **That ceiling counted rows, not places a person can go to.** `kerem-carmel-spice-corner` and `neve-nachum-gutman-museum` are `permanently_closed: true`, so `kerem-hateimanim` and `neve-tzedek` hold **2 open places each, not 3**. The real range is **[2, 2]**. Two consequences, both recorded rather than left implied: 2 is the only admissible value, so the verdict cannot be anything but ACCEPT; and the zero-margin property that disqualified 3 is *already true of 2* in two of three Hoods — which no threshold value can fix, because none smaller is admissible. **The correction lands on the dataset, not the number:** those two Hoods need a third open curated place to restore margin.
- **Spec defects fixed in this same commit (L-009).** The reachability criterion existed in `hood-dataset` req 5 (added at T-047 acceptance) but `passport.md` still contradicted it in two places, so a reader of the owning PRD would still ship the blind version: (1) req 4 gained a pass/fail bullet stating the **rendered consequence** — every designated Hood's row must be reachable to Local by visiting places open today; a row pinned below Local wherever the user goes is a dataset defect, not a UI state; (2) the data-sourcing line's "≥2 curated places" corrected to ≥2 **open** places, naming why the row count is not a guard (it stays green even if every place in a Hood closes). PRD → **Draft v4**. Decision #45 amended in `strategy/decisions.md` with a marked, appended amendment — value unchanged, ceiling corrected, review trigger widened to fire on a **closure**, not only on catalogue growth. `passenger-strategy.md` untouched (Aviran-gated, rule 8).
- **Nothing new filed — the code-side half was already a row.** `PassportBundleInvariantTests.everyDesignatedHoodMeetsThreshold` counts closed places too; that is **T-068/`PAS-64`**, filed 2026-08-05 at T-047 acceptance, still `backlog`/`ios-developer`. Cited rather than duplicated. This pass closes the *spec* half of the same blind spot, which T-068 does not cover.
- **Scope gate (Job 0), run on inherited work:** passes. Passport is V1-authorized verbatim — *"a stamp per Been place, contributing toward 'Local' status **per Hood** in a set of hoods Passenger designates"* (`strategy/passenger-strategy.md`, V1 scope, Passport bullet) — and that same clause puts the parameterisation with the company, which is why #45 was not escalated. No new surface, no social/onboarding/Phase-2 content. Nothing to escalate.
- **Evidence:** `grep -rn "threshold" --include="*.swift" Passenger` (5 hits, 1 declaration); `Passenger/Passport/LocalStatus.swift` read in full; `PassportComposition.progress`/`isOverallLocal` read in full — no `permanentlyClosed` filter, so a Been at a since-closed place still counts (correct: you did go there); `PassengerTests/PassportBundleInvariantTests.swift:28-39` confirms the invariant compares `catalog.places(in:).count`, unfiltered; `python3` derivation over `hoods-tel-aviv.json`/`places-tel-aviv.json`/`place-visits-tel-aviv.json`. **No build or simulator run** — every check is a source or data read, so nothing to preflight; I checked `BOARD.md` for a live build/test `HALT` anyway (L-040) and found only the struck 2026-08-04 one.
- **Side finding, recurrence #4 of L-026/L-027:** `prds/md2html.py` regenerates all PRDs and again dirtied four HTML twins stale on `main` against their own `.md` (`hood-dataset`, `places-been-saved`, `places-dataset`, `search-quick-filters`). Reverted all four rather than sweep them under a T-048 message (rule 2). Already owned — **T-074/`PAS-70`** — so noted, not re-filed.

### 2026-08-07 — ios-code-reviewer — PAS-51 (T-032 orphaned F1/F2 fix): **APPROVE WITH MINORS**

- **Did:** first-ever code review of `passenger-code 927784b` (`HourReadout.swift` gains `.accessibilityIdentifier("hourReadout")`; new `PassengerTests/HeatModalCardLayoutGuardTests.swift` + `PassengerUITests/HeatModalCardLayoutTests.swift`), reviewed against `prds/time-slider/TRD.md` §2.3 (chrome layering) and §9 row 5b (the rendered-frame verification row added at T-055 specifically because F1/F2 slipped past every prior gate). Read `BOARD.md`'s T-032 row + PAS-51 lines and the full F1/F2 history in this file first (2026-08-03 REJECT, 2026-08-04 founder-direct override, 2026-08-04 `c1b8bc3` fixup where the actual `HeatModalCard.swift` constants landed unreviewed as disclosed PAS-15 rode-along content, 2026-08-06 `ios-developer` landing this commit). Also read `HeatModalCard.swift` in full — it is **not** part of this diff (constants `navRowBandHeight`/`gapAboveNavRow`/`maxDynamicTypeSize` already on `main` since `c1b8bc3`) but is what F1/F2's actual fix consists of, so I reviewed it as the effective implementation under test even though this commit doesn't touch it.
- **Independent build+test, not trusted from the commit message:** isolated `git worktree` pinned to `927784b`, dedicated simulator (`pas51-verify`, iOS 26.5, deleted after), dedicated `-derivedDataPath` (deleted after), per L-029. First run of `-only-testing:PassengerUITests/HeatModalCardLayoutTests` **failed** — `hourSlider never rendered` after tapping Heat, at `uptime` load average 407 (four other sessions' concurrent `xcodebuild` processes confirmed via `ps aux`, consistent with this repo's documented shared-machine contention). Diagnosed rather than accepted: reran the identical scoped test alone once load eased — **passed clean, 31.4s**, hourReadout/Heat/Search/Profile frames all resolved and the non-intersection assertion held. Read as environmental flake, not a defect, per this codebase's own established pattern (L-029) — a second, unmodified rerun flipping pass/fail on the same binary is the signature of contention, not of a race in the code under test. The two `HeatModalCardLayoutGuardTests` (Swift Testing) passed cleanly on the first attempt, no retry needed. Independently confirmed the source-level claims: `gapAboveNavRow: CGFloat = 16`, `.padding(.bottom, Self.navRowBandHeight + Self.gapAboveNavRow)`, `maxDynamicTypeSize: DynamicTypeSize = .accessibility3`, `.dynamicTypeSize(...Self.maxDynamicTypeSize)` all present exactly as the guard test's string-match asserts. `MapNavRow`'s three buttons' accessibility labels (`"Heat"`/`"Search"`/`"Profile"`) match what the UI test queries.
- **F1 — genuinely fixed, genuinely verified.** The actual rendered defect (nav row drawing over the readout at default text size) does not reproduce: I watched the real assertion pass against a real rendered frame, independently reproduced, not inherited from the commit's own claimed 518/518.
- **F2 — fixed by a sound mechanism, but under-verified against what §9 row 5b actually demands, and one comment overstates its own evidence.** Findings, none blocking on their own, taken together they're the "minors":
  1. **No live UI-test coverage for AX5 exists anywhere for this surface** — by the commit's own admission (`HeatModalCardLayoutGuardTests.swift`'s doc comment), because `-UIPreferredContentSizeCategoryName` and pre-launch `simctl ui content_size` don't reliably take on this Xcode/iOS build. The substitute is a source-string grep asserting the `.accessibility3` ceiling constant is wired in — which is exactly what row 5b's own verification-method column rules out ("**Not** unit, **not** source review, **not** `exists`"). The substitution is plausible (same construction as `SearchRowGrowthGuardTests`/`PassportAbsenceGateTests` for the identical documented Simulator limitation) but it is a **silent deviation from an explicit TRD row**, never written back into `TRD.md` §9 the way row 5b itself got a T-055 amendment when the original F1/F2 gap was found. Per this repo's own L-009 pattern (three prior instances of "the rule existed, the row didn't"), this should get the same treatment: either amend §9 row 5b to formally accept the structural-guard substitution for the AX5 half (citing the Simulator limitation), or route to `architect`/`product` to decide otherwise.
  2. **`HeatModalCard.swift`'s own comment claims verification evidence that doesn't appear to exist in this repo's audit trail.** `maxDynamicTypeSize`'s doc comment states the AX3 cap was "confirmed by rendering the full … readout at AX3, AX4, and AX5 on device (screenshots in the T-032 rebuild's PR/worklog)." I searched `PROGRESS.md` for any `ios-developer` entry documenting that build pass — none exists; the constants landed inside `c1b8bc3`, explicitly flagged to the reviewer of that commit as PAS-15 content **not to review**, and no dedicated F1/F2 rebuild worklog entry was ever written. The claimed screenshots may be real and simply uncited, but as written this is an unverifiable evidence claim in shipped source, the same shape of problem L-030 exists to catch on the review side.
  3. **The F1 UI test has no positive control**, contra row 5b's explicit standing rule 2 requirement (the pill reads `"next day"` in full, the numeral reads `"+12h"` in full). `.accessibilityElement(children: .combine)` means the three sub-elements aren't individually queryable, which reasonably subsumes row 5b's *per-element intersection* requirement (a non-intersecting bounding box implies no sub-element intersects either) — but it does not substitute for asserting `readout.label`/content, which the shipped test never does.
  4. **The "next day" scenario is wall-clock-dependent, not deterministic.** `MapScreen.swift` calls `now: Date()` directly with no injectable clock reachable from a UI-test launch argument; the test's `slider.adjust(toNormalizedSliderPosition: 1.0)` only lands on an `isNextDay == true` reading when the test happens to run at local hour ≥ 12. It passed this session because it happened to run at 13:33 local; run before noon, it would still assert non-intersection but would never render the "next day" pill — the exact element F1's regression truncated. The docstring's claim to "reproduce product's exact rejection scenario" is only sometimes true.
  5. No runtime assertion of `HeatModalCard.frame.maxY < safe-area bottom` (row 5b's requirement iii, "`bottom: 0` falsified by name") — covered only by the same source-review-style guard test as point 1.
- **Verdict: APPROVE WITH MINORS.** F1, the more severe of the two rejected defects (wrong-hour misreading via truncation), is solidly fixed and independently reproduced passing on a real rendered app. F2 rests on a standard, reliable SwiftUI mechanism (`DynamicTypeSize` range clamping) that is very likely correct even without a fresh live re-check, and the Simulator limitation blocking that re-check is real and precedented elsewhere in this codebase — but the verification trail around it needs to be honest and TRD-conformant rather than implicit. Not blocking because nothing here is an observed regression; routing to `qa` with the five points above as the explicit brief, plus a TRD/comment cleanup nudge back to whoever owns row 5b.
- **Evidence:** `passenger-code 927784b` reviewed in full (`git show`). Isolated worktree build+test: `HeatModalCardLayoutGuardTests` 2/2 passed (first attempt); `HeatModalCardLayoutTests` 1/1 passed (second attempt, first attempt's failure attributed to load average 407 from concurrent sessions, confirmed via `ps aux`/`uptime`). `HeatModalCard.swift` source read in full for the effective F1/F2 implementation. `PROGRESS.md` searched for corroborating F2 on-device evidence — none found.

For `architect`/`product` — decide whether §9 row 5b's AX5 half gets a formal TRD amendment accepting the structural-guard substitution, matching the T-055 precedent. ### 2026-08-07 — product — T-052/`PAS-40` acceptance: ACCEPT (event markers render display names, not raw slugs)

- **Verdict: ACCEPT.** All three scope items verified by re-deriving from source at each repo's HEAD, not from `passenger-code 174a5bb`'s commit message. Task moves to `aviran-review`, per the acceptance protocol — not straight to `done`. `chief-of-staff` owns writing this to `PAS-40` and `BOARD.md`; I touched neither.
- **Item 1 — `map-rendering-spec.md` event-marker rules: PRESENT, and correct against the TRD.** The commit named in the dispatch touches no doc — this item landed in `passenger-brain`, not `passenger-code`, under commit `eea8a23` (labelled for `T-060/PAS-56`, which is why it reads as missing if you only look at `174a5bb`). §2's zoom table now carries a fifth **Event markers** column, filled at all three zoom rows, plus a note below it. Checked the substance, not just the presence: the note states the every-zoom rule and the 12-cap **together, as one decision** ("Uncap it and 'every zoom' stops being safe; drop 'every zoom' and the cap alone doesn't fix the gap… Neither survives being restated alone"), which is exactly what the T-052 filing required and what `prds/live-events-overlay/TRD.md` §4.5 D2 says ("The cap and the zoom rule are one decision; overturning either without the other is wrong"). The spec also names the divergence from its own Pins row explicitly rather than quietly contradicting it. Working tree clean on this file — committed, not stranded.
- **Item 2 — Hood display name: BUILT.** `MapScreen.swift:1174` resolves `hoods.first(where: { $0.id == event.hoodID })?.name` off the Hood list the screen already holds and passes it by value as `EventDetailModal(event:hoodName:)` — no new environment dependency, matching TRD §4.7's shape. `EventDetailModal.swift` renders `Label(hoodName, …)`; the raw `hoodID` no longer appears in any `Label`/`Text` anywhere in `Passenger/` (grepped). It is the only call site.
- **Item 3 — category display label: BUILT.** `EventDetailModal.swift:96` renders `EventDetailRows.displayCategory(category)`, never the raw string. The helper is pure (hyphens/underscores → spaces, `.capitalized`). The reasoning for not using a `displayName` enum is sound and stated in the code: unlike `Place`/`PlaceCategory`, `event.category` has no fixed taxonomy — it's an arbitrary pipeline string per TRD §4.2. Checked it against reality, not just the tests: all 12 category values in the shipped seed fixtures (`art`, `dance`, `eat-drink`, `family`, `film`, `food`, `literature`, `market`, `music`, `outdoors`, `talk`, `things-to-do`) humanize cleanly.
- **Spec defect found and fixed in this same commit (L-009).** The fix introduced a **third rendering state no requirement covered**: the pipeline supplies a `hoodID`, but the client can't resolve it (unknown slug, or Hood set not loaded) — the shipped code omits the row. Req 4's two existing bullets between them don't reach it: one covers "the pipeline did not supply", the other covers "never render a slug". So no gate could have passed or failed the behaviour that actually ships. The omit-on-unresolved choice is the right one (it's the only option that doesn't violate the never-a-slug bullet), but it shipped unspecified. Added a req 4 bullet stating the **rendered consequence** — nothing appears where the row would be: no slug, no blank row, no placeholder, no "Unknown" — not the stored/internal state.
- **Data-need line added in the same pass (Job 1 item 7, the standing 2026-07-30 founder-direct rule, applied to inherited work).** Req 4 now says explicitly that the hood row is renderable only because the client already carries the slug→display-name dataset (`hoods-tel-aviv.json`); this sheet adds no table, field, or fetch of its own. Previously implicit — a developer had to infer it. Also flagged the conditional: if that dataset ever becomes a lazy fetch rather than a bundle, the unresolved-slug bullet stops being an edge case and becomes normal cold-open behaviour, which is a re-decision, not a re-implementation.
- **Scope gate (Job 0), run on inherited work:** passes, and needed no strategy citation of its own — T-052 is a defect fix inside `live-events-overlay`, an already-authorized V1 feature ("Live Events overlay ships in V1 as a third map layer alongside heat + tag"). No new surface, no social/onboarding/Phase-2 content. Nothing to escalate.
- **PRD `Status:` deliberately unchanged** (still "Accepted (Build Phase 1) 2026-08-03 — reqs 1–5 only"). L-006 asks the writer to close the loop when acceptance changes reality; here it doesn't — this is a defect fix against requirements already accepted 2026-08-03, not a new acceptance, and req 6 is still unbuilt. Flipping it would misstate the feature's real state. Decisions-log row added instead, per the shipped-PRD update rule.
- **Evidence:** `git show --stat 174a5bb` (4 files, `EventDetailModal.swift` / `EventDetailRows.swift` / `MapScreen.swift` / `EventDetailRowsTests.swift`); `git log 174a5bb..HEAD --` on all three source files → empty, so nothing reverted it since; `git status --short` in `passenger-code` confirms none of the three is dirty (the only uncommitted files are another session's live `Hoods/`+`Search/` alias work, untouched); `grep -rn "hoodID" Passenger/` shows no user-facing render of the raw id anywhere; `grep -rn "EventDetailModal("` confirms one call site; `git log -S"Event markers" -- design/map-rendering-spec.md` → `eea8a23`; `git status --short` on `design/map-rendering-spec.md` and `prds/live-events-overlay/` clean before my edit. **No build or simulator run** — every scope item is a doc-or-source read, so the L-040 preflight (halt line, `df -h /`, `uptime`, own simulator/derivedDataPath) didn't apply; I checked `BOARD.md` for a live build/test `HALT` anyway and found only the struck 2026-08-04 one.
- **Left behind, non-blocking, not filed as a task:** `displayCategory` uses `.capitalized`, which lowercases the tail of an all-caps token — a future pipeline category `"dj-set"` would render "Dj Set". No shipped fixture value hits this and it's cosmetic, not a req 4 violation (still human text, still not a raw identifier). Worth a line if the real pipeline (`T-043`) ever emits acronyms; not worth a row today.

### 2026-08-07 — developer — Hood alias mechanism (unowned finding, `data-engineer` 2026-08-07): schema built, migration `009`

- **Did:** picked up the unowned finding on `BOARD.md` (`data-engineer`'s Tel Aviv coverage-gap follow-up, `hood-dataset` PRD Decisions log 2026-08-07 "Groups 4/5" entry): 6 real Tel Aviv neighbourhood names have no separate Hood geometry (2 are OSM-identical duplicates, 4 resolve fully inside an already-shipped Hood's polygon) and were unfindable by search because `hoods` had no alias/alt-name mechanism at all. This was flagged explicitly as a schema decision for `developer`, not `data-engineer`, to make.
- **Decision:** `hoods.aliases text[] not null default '{}'` — a column, not a separate `hood_aliases` table. Every confirmed case is a plain same-script alternate name with no per-alias provenance/locale need; a join table is the correct escalation the day that changes, and this column doesn't foreclose adding one later. Full reasoning in `prds/hood-dataset/TRD.md` §8 D11 (new).
- **Built:**
  - `database/migrations/009_hoods_aliases.sql` — additive, `if not exists`-guarded, no RLS change (inherits `001` A2's public-read policy, column-agnostic). Independent of `003`/`006`-`008` — applies in any order relative to them, only needs `001` (the `hoods` table itself).
  - `database/scripts/validate_dataset.py` — `HoodRecord` gains `aliases`; `load_hood_source` parses the optional per-hood `aliases` field (defaults `[]`); new **V10** check: each alias non-empty, no duplicate within a Hood (case-insensitive), no alias collides case-insensitively with any Hood's own `id`/`name` or another Hood's alias anywhere in the dataset. Verified V10 fires correctly against two synthetic bad-data cases (duplicate-within-hood, cross-hood collision) before trusting it on the real 44-row source.
  - `database/scripts/build_hoods.py` — emits `aliases` (text array, `'{}'::text[]` when empty) in both generated artifacts: the migration SQL insert/upsert (`aliases = excluded.aliases` added to the `on conflict` clause) and the iOS bundle JSON. Bundle `schemaVersion` bumped 2 → 3 (decode-compatible in both directions per the TRD's own rule — an unknown higher version is never rejected).
  - `prds/hood-dataset/TRD.md` amended: new top-line `**Amended:** 2026-08-07`, §3.1 (schema), §3.2 (source-file `aliases` field + rule), §3.3 (generated SQL example), §3.5 (bundle v3), §5.2 (V10 row), new **§8 D11** (the decision writeup, including the explicit "`Ohel Moshe` is not a confirmed alias" caveat pulled from `data-engineer`'s later re-investigation). `TRD.html` regenerated via `prds/md2html.py` to match.
  - `database/README.md` — new `009` status-table row; `build_hoods.py`/`validate_dataset.py` description lines updated to mention V10 and the new `009` dependency.
  - `agent-os/BOARD.md` — the unowned finding struck, replaced with what was built and what's still open (population).
- **Explicitly NOT done, and named rather than silently skipped:** no alias data was populated. `hoods-tel-aviv.source.json` is byte-unchanged this pass — no hood carries an `aliases` key yet. `resolve_hood()` was deliberately left untouched — it's point→Hood geometric containment, unrelated to name search; the real name-search consumer is `search-quick-filters/TRD.md`'s client-side `SearchIndex` (`Hood.name` folding today), which needs an `ios-developer` follow-up to also fold `aliases` once real values exist — not built here, this task was schema-only per its own brief.
- **Evidence:** `python3 database/scripts/validate_dataset.py database/data/hoods-tel-aviv.source.json` → `0 error(s), 0 warning(s), 44 hoods` (unchanged, confirming the source itself wasn't touched); `python3 database/scripts/build_hoods.py --check` → same clean result; a full generation run to a scratch path (`--migration-number 999`) confirmed the new `aliases text[]` column/value renders correctly in both the SQL insert (`'{}'::text[]` for every existing row) and the JSON bundle (`"aliases": []`), then deleted; `python3 database/scripts/test_hood_containment_fixture.py` → 9/9 still passing (unrelated to this change, run as a regression check); ad hoc synthetic-data run confirmed V10 fires on both a within-hood duplicate alias and a cross-hood alias/name collision.
- **Left behind for `data-engineer`:** populate `hoods-tel-aviv.source.json`'s new optional per-hood `aliases: string[]` field, then re-run `python3 database/scripts/build_hoods.py --migration-number 010` (next free number) to regenerate the data-seed migration and the iOS bundle together. Six mappings are already confirmed and ready to use: `Kfar Shalem` → `neve-eliezer`, `Ramat Aviv Aleph` → `ramat-aviv`, `Shabazi` → `neve-tzedek`, `Givat Aliyah` → `ajami`, `Neve Tzahal` → `hatikva`, `Yisgav` → `ramat-hachayal` (all six sourced from `hood-dataset.md`'s 2026-08-07 decisions-log rows, cited above). **`Ohel Moshe` must NOT be added** — a later `data-engineer` pass found it has no resolvable OSM entity anywhere in Tel Aviv (only a Jerusalem street match), so it's an unresolved name, not a confirmed alias, until a real source is found. V10 will catch a duplicate/collision mistake at build time either way. Left behind for `ios-developer` (separate, smaller follow-up, not blocking the schema): extend `SearchIndex.build`/`SearchQuery.run` (`search-quick-filters/TRD.md` §4.2) to fold each Hood's `aliases` alongside its `name`. Both migrations (`009`, and whichever number the data-engineer follow-up lands on) still need Aviran to apply them — same gate as every other migration in this directory.
- **Git:** `passenger-brain` — `database/migrations/009_hoods_aliases.sql` (new), `database/scripts/build_hoods.py`, `database/scripts/validate_dataset.py`, `database/README.md`, `prds/hood-dataset/TRD.md`, `prds/hood-dataset/TRD.html`, `agent-os/BOARD.md`, `agent-os/PROGRESS.md` (this entry).

### 2026-08-07 — data-engineer — Tel Aviv Hood gap follow-up (T-040/PAS-17): 15-name gap re-investigated, count stays 44

- **Did:** Aviran-direct follow-up to the previous 24→44 build (`2bb2027`, reviewed `8f76ab8`). Re-investigated the 15 unresolved names left by that pass — 6 unresolvable, 3 overlap conflicts, `Giv'at Herzl`, and 4+2 already-covered/duplicate names — per the five-group brief. No source-file edits: every group's finding is documented, none produced a defensible addition. `database/data/hoods-tel-aviv.source.json` unchanged; `validate_dataset.py` re-run to confirm: `0 error(s), 0 warning(s), 44 hoods`.
- **Method:** Overpass API (`place`-tag search over node/way/relation, broader than Nominatim's indexed text search) via direct `curl`, since network access from this environment reaches OSM/Overpass but not `data.tel-aviv.gov.il`/`opendata.tel-aviv.gov.il` (confirmed unreachable again, `WebFetch` DNS failure and the browser tool's `navigate` denied/failed — same finding as the prior pass). GovMap (`govmap.gov.il`, Hebrew UI) reached successfully via the browser tool and used as a second-source corroboration check for two of the six unresolvable names.
- **Group 1 (6 unresolvable names):** `Migdalei Ne'eman`, `Giv'at Amal Bet`, `Ohel Moshe`, `Shikun Dan` confirmed zero `place=*` OSM entities in the Tel Aviv bbox. GovMap corroborates for the two checked: `מגדלי נאמן` is only a building/institution POI, `אוהל משה` is only a Jerusalem street — neither has a Tel Aviv neighbourhood-layer entry. `Yehuda HaMaccabi` still resolves to streets only. **`Yafo C` is new information**: Overpass found a real municipality-sourced OSM way (`way/819834028`, `"יפו ג' ונוה גולן"` / `"Yafo Gimel & Neve Golan"`, a combined boundary) that the prior Nominatim-only pass missed — but it polygon-overlaps the already-shipped `givat-hatmarim`, so it moves into the same "real entity, no safe automated fix" bucket as group 2, not a clean add.
- **Group 2 (3 overlap conflicts) — quantified, conclusion unchanged:** re-fetched `Tel Haim` (`way/819224104`), `Neve Sharret` (`way/917333960`), `Ramat Aviv HaHadasha` (`way/1008228304`) fresh from OSM and measured the overlaps against the shipped 44 rather than re-asserting the prior finding. `Tel Haim` overlaps two Hoods (`yad-eliyahu`, `ramat-hatayasim`); `Neve Sharret` overlaps `tzahala` with 36 of `tzahala`'s 168 vertices sitting inside it (substantial, not a shared-edge sliver); `Ramat Aviv HaHadasha` overlaps three Hoods at once (`neve-avivim`, `shikun-lamed`, `nofei-yam`). None is the TRD's predicted snap-fixable sliver case (§9) — all three are real interior overlap, so hand-trimming would mean inventing an untraceable boundary. Stays out.
- **Group 3 (`Giv'at Herzl`) — re-investigated, found a second independent blocker:** re-fetched fresh from OSM (`way/817274800`, `source: Tel Aviv municipality`, `wikidata: Q5565569` — confirms it's real and distinct). The central coverage-gap finding reproduces exactly: baseline `coverage_gaps()` on the current 44 is clean (the gap area, roughly lng 34.773–34.786 / lat 32.054–32.063 between `florentin`, `lev-hair` and `kerem-hateimanim`, currently touches the sampled bbox edge and is classified exterior per V7's own design), and adding `Giv'at Herzl` converts it into a ~834,000 m² hard interior error. **New this pass:** `Giv'at Herzl`'s current polygon also independently overlaps `abu-kabir` (5 of `abu-kabir`'s 10 vertices strictly inside it — substantial) and marginally `neve-ofer` (1 vertex each way). Can't tell from here whether this is OSM drift between the two same-day passes or a gap in the prior pass's overlap check (possibly tested only against the original 24, not the full new-candidate batch) — recorded as a live re-verification result either way. Stays out, now for two reasons.
- **Groups 4/5 (8 names, no geometry work) — decision made, no schema built:** confirmed by grep that no alias/alt-name field exists anywhere in `hoods`, the source contract, or either generated artifact. Recommendation only: an `aliases` column or join table is `developer`'s schema call, not built here. Not filed as a Linear `type:data-request` — `linear` MCP unauthenticated in this session (checked directly). Flagged on `BOARD.md`'s Unowned findings for `chief` to route.
- **Evidence:** Overpass/GovMap query transcripts in this session; `polygons_overlap`/vertex-containment checks run against the live `validate_dataset.py` module for all 4 candidate polygons; `validate_dataset.py database/data/hoods-tel-aviv.source.json` → `0 error(s), 0 warning(s), 44 hoods` (unchanged, confirming no drift).
- **Left behind:** full per-name reasoning in `prds/hood-dataset/hood-dataset.md`'s second 2026-08-07 decisions-log row. Nothing here is build-ready — this is a research/investigation pass, not a source-file change. `BOARD.md`'s alias finding needs `chief` routing (Linear issue + `developer`/`architect` schema call) once Linear is reachable. The central coverage gap between the original 4 downtown Hoods is still unfixed and still out of scope — re-surveying it is real boundary-authoring work with no source lined up, same as the prior pass noted.

- **Did:** the real build task the research entry below proposed. Re-fetched all **37** names the research pass had flagged (its own count said 35; recounting the quarter-by-quarter list in that same entry gives 37 — a discrepancy this pass found, not one it introduced) live against OSM Nominatim (`nominatim.openstreetmap.org/search`, same `PassengerApp-DataEngineer/1.0` UA + 1.1s spacing the 2026-07-31 pass used), with a second Hebrew-transliteration retry pass for every name that returned nothing on the first try.
- **Sourcing results, all 37:** 16 resolved to a real, distinct OSM `place=suburb`/`neighbourhood` polygon; 13 resolved to a confirmed-real OSM `place` node with no boundary polygon (hand-drawn-buffer candidates, same fallback category `jaffa` already established); 2 resolved to false-positive matches (a place-of-worship building, a car-repair shop) that aren't the neighbourhood at all; 4 returned nothing at all even after the Hebrew retry.

Re-surveying that pre-existing gap between the original 24 is not this task's scope.
  - 2 more names (`Ohel Moshe`, `Shikun Dan`) had only false-positive matches even after retry, and 4 (`Migdalei Ne'eman`, `Giv'at Amal Bet`, `Yafo C`, `Yehuda HaMaccabi`) had no `place`-tagged OSM entity at all — none invented a boundary for these.

Every new row carries real authored `blurb` text (not `null`, not `[PROVISIONAL]`), `designatedForProgression: false` (no curated places there yet — same gating rule the PRD's existing Decisions log already established), `isTouristTrap: null` (no rating pipeline exists; consistent default with the majority of the original 24).

Value-level diff of every row `007` → `008`: all 24 pre-existing ids byte-identical (only the source-digest comment line and the 20 new rows differ). - **Did:** Aviran asked for full Tel Aviv neighborhood coverage on the heatmap. Read `hood-dataset` PRD (Accepted 2026-08-01, `database/data/hoods-tel-aviv.source.json`) to get the current shipped set — **24 Hoods**: `afeka`, `ajami`, `bavli`, `florentin`, `hadar-yosef`, `hatikva`, `jaffa`, `kerem-hateimanim`, `lev-hair`, `montefiore`, `nachalat-yitzhak`, `neve-avivim`, `neve-eliezer`, `neve-ofer`, `neve-shaanan`, `neve-tzedek`, `old-north`, `ramat-aviv`, `ramat-aviv-gimel`, `ramat-hachayal`, `shapira`, `tzahala`, `tzahalon`, `yad-eliyahu`. Same set backs `passenger-code/Passenger/Resources/hoods-tel-aviv.json` (generated 1:1 by `build_hoods.py`, per the PRD's byte-diff invariant).
- **Compared against a reference list**, not invented: `en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Neighborhoods_of_Tel_Aviv`, which organizes ~60 named neighborhoods by the city's 5 official quarters (North/Trans-Yarkon, Center, Jaffa and South, East, Northeast). Tel Aviv's own open-data GIS portal (`opendata.tel-aviv.gov.il`) and `data.tel-aviv.gov.il` were checked first per the brief but returned no fetchable neighborhood-boundary dataset from this session (portal loaded as a bare SPA shell, no dataset catalog content in the fetch — a session with a live browser/JS-rendering tool should re-check before assuming the portal has nothing; not confirmed absent, only not-reachable this way). GovMap not checked (Hebrew-only mapping UI, needs interactive/browser tooling this pass didn't use).
- **35 named residential neighborhoods are missing** from the shipped 24, none low-confidence — spot-checked one (`Kfar Shalem`) against OSM's Nominatim API live and got a real polygon back (`way/819219046`, combined with the already-shipped `neve-eliezer`'s area), confirming the sourcing method the original 24 used (`hood-dataset` PRD, 2026-07-31 decision log: Nominatim per-place lookup, ODbL-licensed) still works for the gap:
  - **North:** Azorei Hen, Kokhav HaTzafon, Shikun Lamed, Migdalei Ne'eman, Nofei Yam, Ramat Aviv Aleph, Ramat Aviv HaHadasha
  - **Center:** Yehuda HaMaccabi, Tzamarot Ayalon, Giv'at Amal Bet, Shabazi, Ohel Moshe
  - **Jaffa/South:** Yafo C, Giv'at HaTmarim, Givat Aliyah, Kiryat Shalom, Giv'at Herzl, Abu Kabir, American–German Colony
  - **East:** Bitzaron, Ramat Yisrael, Tel Haim, Ramat HaTayasim, Neve Tzahal, Kfar Shalem, HaArgazim, Neve Barbur, Ezra
  - **Northeast:** Ganei Tzahala, HaMishtalah, Ma'oz Aviv, Neve Sharret, Ramot Tzahala, Revivim, Shikun Dan, Tel Baruch, Yisgav

- **No boundary geometry or Hebrew names extracted this pass** — Wikipedia's page gives English names and quarter grouping only, no polygons/Hebrew. Getting real polygons means repeating the 2026-07-31 Nominatim pass (one request per name, 1.1s apart per Nominatim's usage policy, ~35 requests ≈ 40s network time) plus the same manual-fallback judgment calls the original 24 needed (`jaffa`'s hand-drawn polygon, the Old North north/south merge) — not attempted here beyond the one spot-check, since doing it for real means running it through `build_hoods.py`/`validate_dataset.py` properly (non-overlap against all 24 existing Hoods, not just the new 35 against each other) rather than hand-editing the source JSON.

Data-engineer owns it end to end, per this agent's own role file.
- **Proposed next step:** a real build task — source all 35 (Nominatim, same method/attribution as the 2026-07-31 pass, ODbL notice already in the source file's `_attribution` field covers additions), run through `validate_dataset.py` for non-overlap against the full 59-Hood set, regenerate both artifacts via `build_hoods.py`, new migration (`008_hoods_tel_aviv_expansion.sql` or similar, additive only — no `007` row should change). Scoped as its own task rather than folded into this research pass because it touches the same non-overlap invariant `hood-dataset` req 2 made a hard build-failure gate, and because blurb/`is_tourist_trap`/`designated_for_progression` for 35 new rows is real editorial work (req 4/5), not mechanical.
- **Left behind:** this is Aviran-direct with no `T-`/`PAS-` id yet — flagged on `BOARD.md`'s Unowned findings list below rather than invented a task row myself (not `chief`'s lane to allocate is the reverse problem, but allocating one myself risks colliding with `chief`'s own numbering). No source/migration file touched this pass — pure research, nothing to regenerate or diff.
- **Evidence:** `grep -o '"id": "[a-z-]*"'` over `hoods-tel-aviv.source.json` (24 unique ids, matches PRD decision log's 24-count); live `curl` to `nominatim.openstreetmap.org` for `Kfar Shalem, Tel Aviv` (returned a real polygon) and `Ohel Moshe, Tel Aviv` (returned empty — flagging that not every name resolves on the first try, same caveat the 2026-07-31 pass hit with `jaffa`/Old North).

### 2026-08-05 — product — T-038/PAS-29 Search & quick filters acceptance: **ACCEPT** → `aviran-review`, with the row-4a evidence gap ruled sufficient and two items carried

- **Did:** acceptance verdict on T-038. **ACCEPT.** All 8 P0s re-derived from `passenger-code 1903eb1` source independently of `qa`'s round-15 report and of every prior gate. Read workspace-root and `passenger-brain` `CLAUDE.md`, `BOARD.md`'s two T-038 rows (summary + full tracking table) and both banner lines, this file's 2026-08-05 `chief-of-staff` and `qa` entries, then the PRD, TRD (§3.4, §4.9, §4.10, §9, §10, D1/D2/D8/D9/D12/D15) and `TEST-PLAN.md` round 15. Source read from a clean `git archive 1903eb1` extract, **not** the shared working tree — which currently carries another session's uncommitted edits to `Passenger/Map/HoodLayer.swift` and `PassengerTests/HoodLayerFillDimTests.swift`, the two files most load-bearing for this verdict.
- **Scope gate (Job 0), run on inherited work:** authorized verbatim by `strategy/passenger-strategy.md` line 47 — *"**Search + quick filters**, reached from an icon in the map chrome, opening as a sheet. Covers place names, keywords ("hummus", "rooftop bar"), and Hoods… search filters the map, it doesn't bypass it"* — and line 36 for the chips. Quoted in the PRD's Motivation. No social surface, no onboarding, no Phase 2/3 hook: `grep` over `Passenger/Search` + `Passenger/SearchSheet` returns 0 hits for `CoreLocation`/`CLLocation`, 0 for `URLSession`/`PlacesAPI`/`fetch`, 0 for `UserDefaults`/`AppStorage`/`FileManager`, 0 for `openURL`/`UIApplication.shared`, 0 for `fullScreenCover`/`NavigationStack`/`NavigationLink`. Positive control run alongside each (`SearchQuery`/`SearchResultRow` → >0 hits), so the zeroes mean absence, not a wrong path list.
- **The row-4a judgment call, made not inherited.** `qa` proved the dim *mechanism* three ways live but never captured the literal matched-vs-22-others paired frame (camera framing overshot to Rehovot). I ruled it **sufficient**, because the chain the paired frame would demonstrate is readable end-to-end in source and I read every link at the tested commit: `MapScreen.searchEmphasis` (guarded on `chrome.presented == .search`) → `SearchDim.emphasis` (a place match inserts `place.hoodID`, so a match is emphasised at every zoom incl. cold-open, where `PlaceLayer` is gated off by `showsNames`) → `isHoodDimmed` → `HoodLayer(isDimmed:)` → `dimOpacity = isDimmed ? 0.25 : 1` → `borderColor` multiplying it in both branches → `.stroke(borderColor, …)` in the drawn `body`. No inference step anywhere in that chain, and `qa`'s live screenshots independently confirm the border *is* the visible channel at cold open and that search returns exactly the two TRD-expected results end-to-end. The residual risk the frame would have closed — SwiftUI composition swallowing the multiply — is narrow, and a 16th round to re-photograph a chain already read link-by-link is not what the remaining risk is worth.
- **What I did not let pass silently, and it is the real finding: the dim ships with no working automated guard.** At `1903eb1`, `grep -rn "borderColor" PassengerTests/ PassengerUITests/` → **0 hits**, and `borderColor` is still `private` (`HoodLayer.swift:165`). `HoodLayerFillDimTests` asserts `fillColor` exclusively — a value `body` has not read since `PAS-49` (`.foregroundStyle(.clear)` in all three branches). TRD §9 v3's own third standing rule says a row asserting a colour is **unrun, not passed** with a zero coverage anchor, and **BLOCKED** if the named property is `private` at run time — both true today. So the green 460/460 suite proves nothing about req 4 bullet 2, and a total dim regression would keep it green. **TRD step C15 — added at v3 to fix exactly this, explicitly "coverage, not a fix, no production behaviour changes" — was never built.** It is coverage debt against verified-correct behaviour, so it is a follow-up task with an owner, not grounds to bounce a working feature into a 16th cycle. Filed back to `chief-of-staff` as a new task for `ios-developer`, flagged as before-launch.
- **PRD requirement fixed before the verdict, not after (acceptance rule on superseded specs).** Req 4 bullet 2 said *"a non-matching Hood's heat fill renders visibly weaker… dimming only a Hood's 0.5pt stroke and its centroid label is a fail."* `PAS-49` retired Hood fills app-wide on 2026-08-04, after that bullet was written, so read literally the shipped app fails a bullet it satisfies in substance. Rewritten in place to name **whichever channel renders the heat colour** (today the border), with the reasoning inline and a decisions-log row — same commit as the verdict, per L-009/L-006. Status flipped Draft v2 → Accepted in the same commit.
- **The other seven P0s, re-derived at `1903eb1`:** req 1 — zero destination presenters; exactly 2 `.sheet(isPresented:)` sites in view code (`MapScreen:533`, `HoodSheet:44`; the other two grep hits are `DetailRouter` doc comments). Req 2 — `SearchFieldRow` renders a real `Text("Search")` label distinct from the `TextField` prompt; all three match kinds present. Req 3 — `CategoryChipRow` has exactly one call site and iterates `PlaceCategory.allCases` (two cases), selected state carries a `checkmark` glyph + weight change, so it survives greyscale; `minHeight: 44`. Req 5 — no exit of its own. Req 6 — `noMatch` echoes the query verbatim in the message. Req 7 — no persistence surface at all. Req 8 — **round 8's F2 is genuinely fixed**: `CategoryChip`'s `Text` now carries `.fixedSize(horizontal: false, vertical: true)`, the exact modifier TRD §9 row 8(c-ii) names. Second-order gap named: the only test touching that file greps for absent `.lineLimit`, which the PRD and TRD both say is *not* the check — the fix is real but unguarded, same shape as C15, folded into the same follow-up.
- **The two items BOARD.md flags as "flagged for Aviran, not resolved", checked rather than trusted.** (1) **Sheet layout — genuinely still open and correctly headed to him.** Shipped as D2's two heights (`compactFraction 0.45` / `expandedFraction 0.92`, `SearchOverlay.swift:26-27`), i.e. design review's detent recommendation, not Aviran's literal 50/50 ask. Reversible in two constants and one gesture. (2) **Keyword backing field — the row's "now fixed" is accurate, verified not assumed.** `places-dataset` req 4 owns the field; `Place.keywords` is threaded through all three source paths (seed `SeedFile.Entry`, `PlacesAPI.PlaceRow`, `PlacesCache.CachedPlace`); all nine shipped seed places carry ≥1 keyword. **One honest caveat that is not a T-038 defect:** strategy's own probe words "hummus" and "rooftop bar" match nothing in the nine-place Phase-1 seed, so a user typing them today gets the no-match state. Named in TRD §3.4 as not-checkable in Phase 1, owner T-042 step **B4**; the PRD's own line rules a zero-result probe a dataset defect, not a search defect. Mechanism proven with seed keywords that do exist (`shakshuka`, `coffee`, `brunch`).
- **Not run and not claimed:** no `xcodebuild`/simulator run this pass. Preflight per L-040 read `df -h /` and `uptime` and found no active `HALT`, but nothing in this verdict needs a build — every finding is readable in source, and `qa` ran the full unscoped suite green at this exact commit hours earlier. Saying so rather than implying a run I didn't make.
- **Evidence:** `git archive 1903eb1` clean extract; direct reads of `HoodLayer.swift`, `MapScreen.swift` (searchResults/searchEmphasis/isHoodDimmed/isPlaceDimmed), `SearchDim.swift`, `CategoryChipRow.swift`, `SearchFieldRow.swift`, `SearchResultRow.swift`, `SearchEmptyStates.swift`, `SearchOverlay.swift`, `PlaceCatalog.swift`, `places-tel-aviv.json`, `HoodLayerFillDimTests.swift`, `SearchRowGrowthGuardTests.swift`; the eleven negative-existence greps with positive controls; `strategy/passenger-strategy.md` lines 36/47 verbatim; `places-dataset` req 4 + TRD step B4.

### 2026-08-05 — product — T-037/PAS-28 Passport acceptance: **REJECT**, back to `build` (2 findings), plus the missing pass/fail criteria (L-009)

- **Did:** acceptance verdict on T-037/Passport. **REJECT** — 6 of 8 P0s hold; req 3 and req 7 each fail one bullet, same root cause. Read workspace-root and `passenger-brain` `CLAUDE.md`, `BOARD.md`'s T-037 rows (summary + full table) and its struck `HALT` line, this file's 2026-08-05 `qa` entry and the recent `chief-of-staff`/`product` entries, then `prds/passport/passport.md`, `prds/passport/TRD.md` in full, and `TEST-PLAN.md`. Every P0 re-derived from `passenger-code 1903eb1` source, not inherited from `qa`'s PASS WITH MINORS or either code-review APPROVE.
- **Scope gate (Job 0), run on inherited work:** authorized verbatim by `strategy/passenger-strategy.md` line 43 — *"each gets a sticker in your **Passport** shaped to match the place type (coffee cup for a café, etc.), sticker-album style, per city"* — and line 44's *"contributing toward "Local" status **per Hood** in a set of hoods Passenger designates."* No social surface, no onboarding, no Phase 2/3 hook. **Note the authorizing line is itself the evidence for F1:** the strategy states the depiction, not a mapping.
- **F1 — blocking, req 3 bullet 5 ("Sticker shape matches the place's type — a coffee cup for a café", decision #29).** The shipped stickers are abstract geometry: `StickerShape` is `circle, square, triangle, diamond, star, hexagon, generic` with `symbolName` returning `circle.fill`/`triangle.fill`/…, and `Resources/place-types-tel-aviv.json` maps `cafe→circle`, `restaurant→triangle`, `bar→diamond`, `market→square`, `museum→hexagon`, `landmark→star`. A café's sticker is a plain filled circle. That is a bijection, not a match — nothing about a circle says café — and the requirement's own worked example is the pass condition it fails. Verified by reading `Passenger/Places/StickerShape.swift` and the registry at `1903eb1` directly, plus the nine shipped places and their `place_type` values.
- **F2 — blocking, req 7 bullet 1 ("a VoiceOver label naming the place and its type"), and this is the D12 ruling I was asked for.** `PassportLabels.sticker` composes `"\(placeName), \(shape.spokenName) sticker"` over `spokenName` = `"circle"/"square"/"triangle"/…`, so a screen-reader user hears *"Dr. Shakshuka, triangle sticker"*. A geometry word is not a type. **D12's premise is rejected, not confirmed:** it argued req 7 and `places-dataset` §4.4 (*"no user-facing surface renders `place_type`"*) "cannot both be satisfied literally." They can. §4.4 forbids rendering the raw server vocabulary string; it does not forbid naming what a place is in the app's own words. The contradiction existed only because the shape vocabulary was chosen abstract first — a depictive glyph's spoken noun ("cutlery", "cup") names the type in substance while rendering no `place_type` string, satisfying both documents. So D12 is rejected along with its cause rather than ruled on in isolation. It is downstream of F1 but stated separately because fixing F1 alone would still leave the geometry word spoken.
- **Where it was lost, and the L-009 fix made in this same commit:** TRD §9 row 3's pass condition for that bullet is *"every registry key maps to a non-`.generic` case whose `symbolName` resolves"* — totality and symbol resolution, never depiction. So the requirement was weakened at the TRD layer and no gate downstream had anything objective to fail it on; `qa` correctly reports PASS against the checks it was handed. Per L-009 the missing criteria were written in the same commit as the verdict, stated as the rendered consequence and labelled as added at acceptance: `passport` req 3 gained a name-hidden depiction test that explicitly fails a distinct-but-abstract shape per type, and req 7 gained the *"cutlery sticker" passes / "triangle sticker" fails* bullet. `places-dataset` req 3 gained the matching bullet in the same pass (Job 0 item 4 — its own two bullets guarantee coverage and say nothing about what the shape shows, which is the upstream half of the same blind spot, and B1's ratified vocabulary is where the pairing is fixed). `passport` → Draft v3; decisions-log rows on both PRDs.
- **Not a scope or taste call, and no founder ratification is being overruled:** the registry's own `_note` says PROVISIONAL pending Aviran's T-042 B1, so no ratified vocabulary exists yet. But B1 ratifies the JSON's *values*; the six abstract cases live in client Swift (`StickerShape`), so no ratification can turn "circle" into a cup — the fix is a code change in T-037's own C2, not a wait on T-042. Specific glyph choices stay provisional under B1 exactly as today.
- **The other six P0s hold, re-derived independently at `1903eb1` (`git archive`, not the shared dirty tree — several sessions have WIP in it, incl. `MapScreen.swift`):** req 1 — zero hits across all nine social/account/network symbols, zero non-`systemName` `Image(`, zero `UserDefaults`/`FileManager`; req 2 — `onProfileTap: openPassport` wired at `MapScreen.swift:526`, zero `camera`/`selectedHour` reads in `Passport/`; req 4 — `progress` filters `designatedForProgression` so undesignated Hoods are absent not zero, `isOverallLocal` guards the empty set explicitly rather than relying on `allSatisfy`, zero retired tier names, exactly one `threshold` declaration (`LocalStatus.threshold = 2`); req 5 — `PassportProgressList` renders `Text("\(beenCount) of \(threshold)")` always plus a `Label("Local")` word-and-glyph, empty state is icon + one line, no bar/lock/teaser; req 6 — zero interruption presenters, no observer on `visits`; req 8 — zero `CoreLocation`/authorization/network symbols. Req 3's other bullets (Been-only, one-per-place, revisit-adds-none, no shapeless sticker, one city page) all hold — only the depiction bullet fails.
- **Not run and not claimed:** no `xcodebuild`/simulator run this pass. Preflight per L-040 read `df -h /` 63Gi avail/16% used (fine) but `uptime` load **32.90 / 55.29 / 43.40** — abnormal, concurrent sessions building — and L-040 says a result seen under abnormal load isn't trustworthy, so I did not start a build. Nothing in this verdict needs one: both findings are readable in source, and `qa` already ran the full unscoped suite green (485/485) at this exact commit. `qa`'s two named minors (greyscale and airplane-mode live captures) are accepted as structurally proven and are **not** part of this rejection.
- **Evidence:** `git show 1903eb1:` on `StickerShape.swift`, `PassportLabels.swift`, `PassportStickerView.swift`, `PassportProgressList.swift`, `PassportComposition.swift`, `PassportAlbum.swift`, `LocalStatus.swift`, `ProfileButton.swift`, `place-types-tel-aviv.json`, `places-tel-aviv.json`; grep sweep over a clean `git archive` extract of `1903eb1`; `strategy/decisions.md` #29 read verbatim; TRD §9 row 3 and D12 read in full.
- **Board/Linear:** not touched by this agent — `chief-of-staff` is sole writer (L-038), and this run's dispatcher (`main`) posts both. T-037 should move `acceptance` → `build`, `owner:product` → `owner:ios-developer`. PRD Status flipped to Draft v3, **not** to Accepted — the Job 3 flip rule applies to ACCEPT only.

Not pushed — Aviran-gated, `CLAUDE.md` rule 9. Hash in the final report.

### 2026-08-05 — product — T-047/PAS-34 acceptance: ACCEPT, plus the missing pass/fail criterion (L-009)

- **Did:** acceptance verdict on T-047 (`designated_for_progression` authored for all 24 Hoods). **ACCEPT.** Read workspace-root and `passenger-brain` `CLAUDE.md`, `BOARD.md`'s T-047/T-048 rows and its struck `HALT` line, and this file's recent T-047 entries before starting. Every P0 re-derived from source independently rather than inherited from `qa`'s PASS WITH MINORS or `code-reviewer`'s APPROVE. **Scope gate (Job 0) run on inherited work:** authorized verbatim by `strategy/passenger-strategy.md` line 44 — *"contributing toward "Local" status **per Hood** in a set of hoods Passenger designates"*. No social/onboarding/Phase-2 surface; the task parameterises an already-authorized V1 mechanic. **Data/schema need (Job 1 item 7):** already explicit — `hood-dataset` req 5 owns the field and now owns its validation criterion too.
- **Evidence — re-derived, not inherited:** `hoods-tel-aviv.source.json` and the shipped `passenger-code/Passenger/Resources/hoods-tel-aviv.json` both hold 24 Hoods with exactly `florentin`/`kerem-hateimanim`/`neve-tzedek` designated; compared as a **per-id flag map**, not just a count, and the id sets are equal. Note the key is camelCase `designatedForProgression` in both JSON artifacts and snake_case only in SQL — a snake_case grep over the JSON returns nothing and reads as "field absent", which is a trap for the next agent checking this. `006`→`007` diffed **row-by-row with polygons masked** (`re.sub` on the geometry literal): 24 rows each, same ids, 6 rows changed — the 3 designation flips (`false`→`true`, last column) plus 3 unrelated `is_tourist_trap` `null`→`false` edits on `montefiore`/`old-north`/`shapira` (T-057's fixture work). Column order confirmed from the `insert into` header, so "last column" is verified as `designated_for_progression` rather than assumed. Upsert refreshes every mutable column incl. `updated_at`; the trailing `delete` is scoped to `city = 'tel-aviv'` **and** to the 24 authored ids. `places-tel-aviv.json`: 9 places, 3/3/3 in exactly the designated Hoods, 0 in the other 21 — the set is forced by data. `LocalStatus.threshold == 2` read directly. `PassportBundleInvariantTests` read in full. **Not run and not claimed:** no `xcodebuild` test run this pass (no code changed by this verdict; the earlier gates' green-run gap is carried, not closed here), and no live-DB check — migration `007` is unapplied, Aviran-gated.
- **The one substantive finding, fixed in this same commit per L-009:** the designated set had **no falsifiable criterion tied to what a user can actually reach** — only to how many rows exist. `PlaceCatalog.places(in:)` does not filter `permanentlyClosed` (confirmed by reading it), so `everyDesignatedHoodMeetsThreshold` counts closed places. `kerem-hateimanim` (`kerem-carmel-spice-corner`) and `neve-tzedek` (`neve-nachum-gutman-museum`) each carry one permanently-closed place of three, so both sit at **2 visitable of a threshold of 2 — zero margin today**, and the invariant would stay green even if every place in a designated Hood closed, with Local unreachable in-app. That is the L-009 shape exactly: the check reads a stored count, not the rendered consequence. Added as a new pass/fail bullet on `hood-dataset` req 5, labelled as added at acceptance, plus a Decisions log row. **This is not a defect in the authored values** — they are the only set the data permits — so it does not block the ACCEPT.
- **Left behind:** (1) **`ios-developer`** — retarget `PassportBundleInvariantTests.everyDesignatedHoodMeetsThreshold` at the *visitable* count (`permanentlyClosed == false`), so the new req-5 bullet has a test behind it; needs a `BOARD.md` row with an owner, not a worklog line (L-026/L-027). Read every file against the TRD (§4.1–§4.10, §9, §10) before touching anything. Found and fixed six real defects while finishing what C6/C8 needed, none inherited from any report:
  1. **A3's own defect, still present:** the camera bottom inset was still `visibleAboveSheetFraction: Double = 0.48`, hardcoded from `qa`'s single iPhone-17 measurement — the exact thing amendment A3 exists to forbid ("no device-specific pixel constant anywhere in the fit path"). Replaced with two `onGeometryChange`-measured heights (map viewport, presented sheet), a `nil`-fraction zero-inset fallback before either is known, and a one-time re-fit (`hasFitWithMeasuredSheetHeight`) when the real height arrives after the first fit — matching A3's stated edge case exactly. `git grep` for `415|866|451|iPhone` in the fit path now returns 0 hits (one unrelated pre-existing "iPhone" in an `.onContinuousHover` comment, not camera/inset-related).
  2. That change pushed `MapScreen.body` — already at the type-checker's patience limit — into "unable to type-check this expression in reasonable time," a genuine build failure. Fixed with a monomorphized `private extension View { func measuringHeight(...) }` helper and extracting the `.sheet` content into `depth1SheetContent()`.
  3. `visibleAboveSheetFraction`/`fittedRegion` needed explicit `nonisolated` — without it, a nonisolated test context fails to compile against them ("main actor-isolated default value in a nonisolated context"). Root cause not fully resolved — pre-existing `isNewGrant`/`regionDump` on the same struct compile fine with no annotation — but the explicit keyword is correct and unambiguous regardless of the underlying inference rule.
  4. `RouteControls`' text runs (both button labels, the disclosure line) had no `.fixedSize(horizontal: false, vertical: true)` — C5's own build step and §9 row 15c name this explicitly for AX5 no-truncation. Added throughout.
  5. Three `Routing/` doc comments said "MKMapItem"/"MKDirections"/"MKRoute" in prose outside the one file §9 row 13(b) permits (`WalkingRouteProvider.swift`) — the row's grep ("exactly 1 file") failed on comment text with zero actual behavior at fault. Reworded to describe the type without the literal token; re-verified the grep now returns exactly 1 file.
  6. `PassengerTests/ScenicWaypointPlannerTests.swift` called its own `private static` helpers (`squareHood`, `pointNearRoute`, `makePlace`, `straightRoute`) as bare names from instance `@Test` methods throughout — Swift requires `Self.` from instance context, so this was a real compile error, not a style gap. Confirms this file had literally never been built before. Fixed every call site.

- **Did:** acceptance pass on `passenger-code b5f2fda` ("Nav bar icons — bigger, colored, unique chrome") against `designer`'s spec (`design/phase-1/nav-bar-icons-redesign.md`) and Aviran's founder-direct request (this file's 2026-08-04 "L-002 stub: founder-direct request, nav bar icons" entry). - **L-013 check on the premise, done first:** the L-002 stub's verbatim quote is **"i want the nav bar icons will be bigger and with colors, unique."** Everything downstream — designer's "no two adjacent buttons read the same," the dispatch brief's "visually unique per button" — is a gloss on that one word, not in Aviran's own text. D7 now describes the shipped nav-row button and names the guard as what replaced the fade — **and says why the replacement is stronger**: a fade is a visibility state a layout change can undo, a guard is a branch. Also records that T-032's D1 needed no correction from this task after all.
- **D8's rationale rewritten; D8's rule untouched.** The old text justified the decision by claiming `.presentationBackgroundInteraction(.enabled(upThrough: .medium))` "makes bucket-2 chrome tappable while a sheet is up… both orderings are reachable states." It does not — it keeps the underlying state interactive rather than inert, which is a claim about reachability once the sheet moves, not about hit-testing through it. The rewrite rests the decision on the two enforcing calls, and splits the two orderings honestly: leaving `.places` with a stacked modal **is** tap-reachable (and is covered by `PlacesListInteractionTests.testDismissingStackedPlaceModalRevealsListThenLeavingPlacesOpensHeat`, passing at `passenger-code 5e1f72f`), while a nav tap under an open depth-1 sheet is **not**, so `closeHood()` in `openPlacesList` is a structural guarantee rather than a fix for a live defect. Named as a pre-existing reasoning error, not a PAS-42 regression — it was equally wrong at the old bucket-2 position.

Read workspace-root `CLAUDE.md`, `passenger-code/CLAUDE.md`, this file's eight prior T-062/PAS-58 entries (both my own REJECTs, the loop-guard escalation, Aviran's new direction, round 3) and `BOARD.md`'s T-062 row + struck HALT banner first. **Every number below is my own live measurement — nothing inherited from the round-3 report.** Own `df -h /` at session start (65Gi avail) and again immediately before each build; no other `xcodebuild` or booted simulator at start. Isolated detached worktree at `f6ee513`, dedicated `-derivedDataPath`, dedicated simulator (`t062-r4-accept`, erased → booted → installed → `simctl privacy grant location-always` → launched), all four temp edits worktree-only.
- **Verdict: ACCEPT.** Both bars that produced rounds 1 and 2 now pass, and the founder's original complaint is answered rather than traded off.
- **Req 1 legibility bullet — PASSES at 90.5%**, floor is ≥50%. `EventMarker` RGB (45,27,105) tol 20: **2,914px** at the true on-centroid Florentin coordinate (seed-0001 sits *exactly* on the Florentin centroid — verified in `hoods-tel-aviv.json`, both `[34.76928, 32.057561]`) against **3,220px** for the same marker on a clean patch. Reproduces designer's 2,914 exactly on the numerator.
- **The 92.5% figure was slightly optimistic, and the cause is a spec-method defect, not a designer error.** Req 1's bullet said to compare against "the same marker rendered clear of any label." Displacing the event ~220m north clears the *Hood label* but lands it beside an Apple Maps *POI pin*, which clips the reference — so the **divisor** is too small. My first reference reproduced designer's almost exactly (3,146 vs their 3,149 → 92.6% vs their 92.5%), then failed its own symmetry check: left/right halves 1,640/1,506, R/L **0.918**, against **0.982** for the occluded marker whose clipping is horizontal and therefore x-symmetric. Re-measured on a programmatically-chosen quiet patch: 3,220px, R/L **0.984** — genuinely unclipped. **Fixed in the PRD this commit (L-009):** req 1's bullet now requires the reference be clear of any label, pin *or other map annotation*, and confirmed unclipped before dividing by it.
- **Req 4 tappable-ink bullet — PASSES, and the test discriminates.** Wrote a temp XCUITest tapping absolute screen points measured off this build's own pixels (topmost ink **(200.7, 446.0)pt**, bottommost **(200.7, 468.0)pt**). Both open the event sheet, `hoodSheetTitle` absent, title asserted `"Rooftop set, Levinsky"`. **Added a negative control** so this couldn't pass vacuously — a tap 60pt below the bottommost ink, still inside the Hood polygon, opens the **Hood** sheet as expected. All three pass. (Simulator-control MCP needs interactive approval unavailable here; XCUITest is the stronger check anyway — it asserts *which sheet*, not a screenshot.)
- **Aviran's original complaint, judged not just pass/failed.** "Too big… more solid and small" is answered. Drawn ink is **~358pt²** (3,220px ÷ 9) against the original 44pt marker's ~1,900pt² — an **81% reduction** — and the bbox is a symmetric **26.7 × 26.7pt**, against round 2's 22 × 82pt barbell. It is one filled shape with one glyph: a single gestalt that points at a spot, which is exactly what rounds 1 and 2 lacked. Round 2's REJECT reason (d) is also resolved — at cold-open city-wide zoom the marker is compact and proportionate, no longer the most dominant object on screen.
- **Locali fidelity verified against the actual source, not the description.** Pulled `Locali/Features/Map/EventMarker.swift` from `github.com/AviranGrisaro/locali` myself (sparse clone). The match is near-literal: same 22×22 `RoundedRectangle` rotated 45°, same `Image(systemName: "calendar")` at `.system(size: 12, weight: .semibold)` in white, same 34pt halo, same `shadow(radius: 2, y: 1)`. Documented differences are deliberate and minor — `cornerRadius` 5 continuous vs 3, halo drawn as a diamond rather than Locali's circle, and round 2's scale+fade curve kept over Locali's opacity-only pulse. This is the icon Aviran pointed at.
- **Also verified live, not code-read:** Reduce Motion — two shots 1s apart with RM **off** diff across a 94×94px region (max channel delta 90, the halo); with RM **on**, `getbbox()` → `None`, zero motion, and the core measures **3,220px — byte-identical to RM-off**, which also confirms the tol-20 method never counted halo pixels. Full `PassengerTests`: **401 tests / 71 suites passed** (independently reproduces designer's claim). Existing `EventDetailSheetInteractionTests` still passes — the `anchor:` change didn't break the accessibility-element tap path.
- **One accepted trade-off, named not buried.** `markerVerticalNudge` draws every marker 18pt below its true coordinate at *all* zooms, including where no Hood label exists. Geographic error is ~42m at the 0.01° test span and ~800m at cold-open city-wide — but it **shrinks as the user zooms in**, so precision improves exactly where it starts to matter. Benign, already documented in-code by `designer`, and not a REJECT basis. Flagging for Aviran rather than deciding alone whether it's worth the machinery to gate on zoom tier.
- **PRD `Status:` deliberately not flipped** (contra the usual L-006 step): this PRD is already `Accepted (Build Phase 1) 2026-08-03` and T-062 is a post-ship redesign against reqs accepted then, not a new feature acceptance. Flipping it would misstate what happened. Decisions-log row added instead.
- **Not mine, unchanged:** `florentin`/`music` still render raw in the event sheet (visible in every screenshot) — T-052/PAS-40's known uncommitted work, not a T-062 regression.
- **Evidence:** 4 `xcodebuild build` runs (all SUCCEEDED), 3 `xcodebuild test` runs. Screenshots/crops session-local, not committed. **Cleanup confirmed:** simulator deleted, worktree removed, derived data removed, temp test file deleted; `passenger-code` HEAD still `b5f2fda` and its dirty set is other sessions' WIP only — this session committed nothing to `passenger-code`.
- **Left behind:** ready for `aviran-review` — this is the round Aviran should actually look at, since the two prior rejections were mine and his new direction is what unblocked it. The 18pt nudge trade-off above is the one open item worth his call.

Disk stayed healthy throughout (42-66Gi avail, never near the 5GB floor) but the **machine hit severe CPU contention mid-session** — `uptime` load average peaked at **977** (normal is single digits) from other sessions' concurrent `xcodebuild test` runs, confirmed via `ps aux` showing another session's live compile (`wt-t062r4` worktree) and `t038-round14`'s own disk-guarded test run. Not a disk HALT condition (per L-034's own trigger), so proceeded rather than stopping, but flagging the load-average dimension explicitly since today's documented pattern was disk-only — this is a second, distinct contention axis worth watching for.

- **(a) All 5 buttons render distinctly — confirmed, not just "it compiled."** Built clean (`xcodebuild build`, BUILD SUCCEEDED), ran on `t064-qa-iphone16`, screenshotted the live nav row over real Tel Aviv map tiles. Pixel-sampled each glyph's rendered color: Heat `(255,141,40)`≈systemOrange, Search `(0,136,255)`≈systemBlue, NearMe `(52,199,89)`=exact systemGreen (active/granted state), Places `(255,45,85)`=exact systemPink. Independently re-read all 5 button source files myself (not just trusting the prior code-review report) — `HeatButton`/`SearchButton`/`ProfileButton`/`NearMeButton`/`PlacesButton` all match the design spec's code block exactly, including `NearMeButton`'s active/denied color+glyph swap together. `git show --stat b5f2fda` independently confirmed only the 5 button files changed, `MapNavRow.swift` untouched.

Flagged as a background task (`task_a7b879cd`, not filed to Linear myself — `chief-of-staff` is the sole Linear writer) recommending the fixed-sleep synchronization be replaced with a real signal.

All 4 checks the dispatch named came back clean; the one test failure encountered was triaged to a pre-existing timing-flake unrelated to this diff, not a P0 fail.
