### 2026-08-03 — product — T-038/PAS-29 acceptance pass: **REJECT** — req 4's dim emphasises nothing at the app's own cold-open zoom, and no test anywhere asserts a layer's dim

- **Did:** acceptance pass on T-038 (Search & quick filters) against `prds/search-quick-filters/search-quick-filters.md` and `TRD.md` v1, dispatched after `ios-code-reviewer` APPROVE WITH MINORS and `qa` PASS. Read `BOARD.md`'s two T-038 rows and the `qa` entry below before starting. **Re-derived all eight P0s from `passenger-code` source directly**, per this workspace's standing acceptance discipline — not a re-read of the two upstream verdicts.
  - **Commit tested:** `passenger-code 3d8e3b8`. The brief's "confirmed unchanged by `beb3f02`" was already stale on arrival — `passenger-code 2267090` (`qa`'s own new tests) and `passenger-code 198b082` (T-032's time-slider build) have since landed, and **198b082 rewrites `MapScreen.swift` (+264/−) and `MapNavRow.swift`**, both search-carrying files. Re-derived against current `main` as well as the pinned commit; the search wiring survived T-032 intact (`SearchOverlay` at z5, z3 tap-catcher at `MapScreen.swift:332`, `MapNavRow` search button, `isDimmed` on both layers all present and correct after the merge). No T-032 regression on T-038. Worth stating because a pinned-hash brief plus a fast-moving `main` is how a verdict ends up describing a tree nobody is running.
  1. **Seven of eight P0s hold**, each re-derived rather than accepted: req 1 (one door — `openSearch`/`dismissSearch`/`handlePresentedSurfaceChange` read directly, `grep` for `fullScreenCover|NavigationStack|NavigationLink` over `Search/`+`SearchSheet/` → 0); req 2 (three match kinds — `SearchQuery.run`'s five rules read line by line, visible `Text("Search")` label distinct from the `TextField` prompt); req 3 (two chips, one call site, `.fresh == .all`, checkmark-not-colour selected state); req 5 (routing — both branches call the same `router.openPlace`/`openHood` a pin or polygon tap makes; exactly two real `.sheet(isPresented:` sites app-wide, the other two `grep` hits being `DetailRouter`'s own doc comments); req 6 (four states present; `grep` for `CoreLocation|LocationStore|CLLocation` → 0, so location-denied is structurally a no-op); req 7 (session lives on `MapScreen` as `@State` and `handlePresentedSurfaceChange` takes no `session` parameter — an interruption *cannot* clear it, Poka-Yoke rather than a tested behaviour; all four manual-dismiss paths verified by call site to route through the one `dismissSearch`); req 8 partially — 44pt frames and both pinned VoiceOver strings are real and correct.
  2. **F1 — BLOCKING, PRD req 4 bullet 1. Two compounding halves; together, opening search and typing a match changes nothing a user can see.**
     - **F1a — the Hood dim never reaches the heat fill.** `HoodLayer.dimOpacity` is applied to the polygon's *stroke* (`.opacity(0.35 * dimOpacity)` / `Color("Flag").opacity(dimOpacity)`) and to `annotationContent`, but `MapPolygon.foregroundStyle(fillColor)` is left untouched in all three `effectiveStroke` branches, and `HeatPalette.fill(for:)` returns `hue.opacity(opacity(for: band))` — a fixed per-band alpha with no dim factor. So a non-matching Hood renders its **full-strength heat fill** while search is open. The fill is the dominant visual and the channel req 4 bullet 1 is actually about; what dims is a 0.5pt hairline and a label that is invisible at city-wide zoom anyway. The file's own doc comments assert the opposite **twice** — *"`true` multiplies every visible element's opacity by 0.25"* and *"the one multiplier every visible element in this layer applies"* — so this reads as intended behaviour that was never wired, not a deliberate scoping call.
     - **F1b — a place-only match has no pin to emphasise at the zoom the app opens at.** `MapScreen.swift:244` gates the `PlaceLayer` `ForEach` on `showsNames`, i.e. `zoomTier == .close`, i.e. latitude span **< 0.06**. The shipped cold-open camera is `Self.telAvivCityWide`, span **0.14** → `.cityWide` → **no place pin is constructed at all**. `SearchDim.emphasis` returns `(places: {ids}, hoods: [])` for such a match, so no Hood is emphasised either.
     - **Rendered result at cold open, F1a + F1b together:** heat fills unchanged, hairline strokes marginally fainter, no pins. The map is visually indistinguishable from before the search. Reproduce in ~15 seconds: cold open → search → type `"suzan"` (2 place results, 0 Hood results, both confirmed by `SearchShippedSeedTests`). **Surface:** F1a at every zoom and every query; F1b at every zoom with span ≥ 0.06 (`.cityWide` **and** `.neighborhood`) — the app's opening state for everyone and the *permanent* state for a user who denies location, which PRD req 6 bullet 5 explicitly supports. Not a corner case; it is the primary path for the most common query type.
     - **Correction, disclosed rather than quietly rewritten.** The first version of this entry (`passenger-brain 8545d2b`) described F1 as *"every Hood polygon/stroke/annotation at 0.25 opacity, zero pins, nothing bright."* That was **wrong** — it took `dimOpacity`'s doc comment at its word instead of tracing `fillColor` through `HeatPalette`. The verdict does not change and the requirement still fails, but the mechanism is close to the inverse of what was first written, and F1a is the larger half of the defect. Caught on this agent's own critic pass before delivery (`CLAUDE.md`'s writing rules), corrected in this entry's follow-up commit (hash in the Git line below). Worth recording as its own small lesson: I rejected this build for asserting a passed value instead of a rendered one, and then made the same class of mistake in the rejection by trusting a doc comment instead of the render path.
  3. **Why five gates passed it, and the fix that outlives this task (L-009).** TRD §9 row 4(a)'s pass condition is *"that place's id and that Hood's id are the only members; every other `PlaceLayer`/`HoodLayer` receives `isDimmed == true`"* — every clause of which is **true in the failing case**. It asserts the value handed to a layer, never what the layer draws. And it was never implemented anyway: `grep -rn "isDimmed" PassengerTests/ PassengerUITests/` returns **0**, and `grep` for `showsNames|zoomTier|cityWide` across all five search test files returns **0**. So the requirement had no falsifiable check at any layer, and `SearchDimTests` (green, 3 cases) tests set membership on the pure function while the composition that breaks sits in `MapScreen`. Per L-006/L-009 I wrote the missing criteria into the PRD **in this same commit**, stated as the rendered consequence: req 4 gains *"whenever results show, at least one map element renders undimmed — at every zoom the map can be at, including the cold-open zoom"*, and req 8 gains the chip case. Both labelled as added at acceptance with the reason.
  4. **F2 — non-blocking, fold into the same fix pass, PRD req 8 bullet 3.** `CategoryChipRow` is a fixed `HStack` with no wrap and no horizontal scroll, and neither chip's `Text(category.displayName)` carries `.fixedSize`. At the largest accessibility size `.subheadline` scales roughly 15pt → ~42pt; "Eat & Drink" and "Things to do" plus two checkmarks plus 28pt of horizontal padding each is well over 600pt against a ~393pt width, so both labels compress and truncate. `qa`'s new `SearchRowGrowthGuardTests` sweeps for `.lineLimit(` and correctly finds none — but `.lineLimit` is not the mechanism here; `HStack` overflow is, and only `.fixedSize` prevents it. `SearchResultRow` has `.fixedSize` on both `Text` runs; `CategoryChip` has it on neither. **Stated as a code-read finding, not a live one** — `qa` could not force a content-size override in that session and neither could I, so this is reasoning from the layout, not an observed screenshot.
  5. **Four `architect` questions were routed to `product` at TRD §12 and are all ruled**, in the PRD's Decisions log: **D8 upheld** (a chip narrows *places*; a Hood match survives — req 3 says "narrows," a Hood carries no `PlaceCategory`, and dropping Hoods would make a chip delete a whole result kind); **D9 upheld** (two row words; a keyword hit is a Place row — req 2 bullet 2's own justification clause is a two-way distinction and req 8's VoiceOver examples give only two forms); **§4.10's undimmed MapKit base tiles accepted as a limitation** (the requirement is about the prominence of Passenger-authored content; `Map` exposes no layer to insert one into and a full-screen scrim would darken the matches); **D2 (0.45/0.92) accepted for Build Phase 1 and carried to Aviran**, whose call the layout is (§9 Q15) — the architect priced the reversal at two constants and one gesture, so nothing is locked. **Not ruled:** D10's underlying interrupted-vs-completed product rule (`ux-flows.md` §9 Q17) — flagged by `designer` as its own reconciliation rather than Aviran's call, so it stays Aviran's and is not `product`'s to close alone. The build matches the PRD as written either way.
  6. **Scope gate re-run on inherited work** (Job 0 item 4 — a task already in the pipeline gets the citation check the first time `product` touches it at a gate). Both Motivation quotes verified **verbatim** against `strategy/passenger-strategy.md`: the "Search + quick filters… search filters the map, it doesn't bypass it" line at strategy line 47, and the "surfaced as **quick filters** inside the search sheet" line at line 36. Authorized V1 scope. No social surface, no onboarding, no Phase-2/3 hooks anywhere in `Search/` or `SearchSheet/`. **Data/schema need** (Job 1 item 7): already explicit and already owned — `places.keywords` is specified in `places-dataset` req 4 with the probe list as T-042 step B4, and the PRD's Technical design states the three matched fields by name. Nothing to fix on that pass.
- **Verdict: REJECT.** Back to `build`, `ios-developer`. Fix F1; fold in F2. Fix *direction* deliberately not prescribed — emphasising a matched place's own Hood is the obvious candidate (`SearchIndex.PlaceEntry` already carries both `hoodName` and `place.hoodID`, so the data is in hand), but ungating matched pins from `showsNames` or moving the camera are live alternatives and the call is `architect`/`ios-developer`'s.
- **Evidence:** all greps and line numbers above run against `passenger-code` current `main` and cross-checked at `3d8e3b8`; `git show --stat 3d8e3b8`, `git diff --stat 3d8e3b8..HEAD`. No build or test run was needed to establish F1 — it follows from two constants in shipped source (`MapZoomTier.closeSpanThreshold = 0.06`, `telAvivCityWide` span `0.14`) and one `if` at `MapScreen.swift:244`.
- **Left behind:**
  - **`architect` owes a TRD §9 amendment** — filed as **T-053** on `BOARD.md` with `architect` as owner rather than left in this entry (`CLAUDE.md` rule 5): row 4(a) and row 8(c) both assert a passed value instead of a rendered result. **Flagged as a class, not an instance** — this is L-009's second occurrence after T-046/LOC-104, so the sweep across other shipped TRDs' §9 tables matters more than the two rows.
  - **The live visual dim is still unconfirmed by a human**, carried from `qa`'s entry below — and F1 is precisely the kind of thing a 30-second look at a real device would have caught before code review. Worth noting that the environment gap and the escaped defect are the same gap.
  - `TEST-PLAN.md` row 4 will need rebuilding once the amended TRD §9 lands; I did not edit `qa`'s artifact.
- **Git:** `passenger-brain` — `prds/search-quick-filters/search-quick-filters.md` (req 4 + req 8 bullets, 6 Decisions-log rows, Status → Draft v2), `agent-os/BOARD.md` (both T-038 rows + new T-053 row), `agent-os/PROGRESS.md` (this entry). Explicit paths, two commits: **`passenger-brain 8545d2b`** (verdict) then a follow-up correcting the F1 mechanism (see item 2's last bullet — the second commit exists because the first was wrong, and says so rather than being squashed away). **Rule-2 note:** the BOARD.md half of that correction was swept into `passenger-brain 92b4b3d`, a concurrent T-037/PAS-28 session's commit, before this agent could stage it — same shape as the `0dd3d21` incident `CLAUDE.md` rule 2 documents. Named here rather than silently absorbed; nothing of that session's was carried the other way by this agent. Committed, **not pushed** — Aviran-gated, `CLAUDE.md` rule 9.   - **C2** — `Passenger/Map/HeatButton.swift` (new, `flame.fill`, icon-only per D6, mirrors `SearchButton`/`ProfileButton`'s 44×44 `.thinMaterial` circle convention exactly) added to the already-existing `MapNavRow.swift` (which only had Search/Profile from concurrent builds) rather than recreating the row. Bucket-2 fade (`NearMeButton`/`HoodButton`/`SettingsHint`) wired in `MapScreen.swift` via a shared `.opacity(chrome.isPresenting ? 0 : 1).allowsHitTesting(!chrome.isPresenting)` on their existing container — the TRD's own §2.3 comment in `MapScreen.swift` had explicitly deferred this to "T-032's own build."
  - **C3-C5** — `Passenger/HeatModal/{HourFormat,HourSlider,HourReadout,HeatModalCard}.swift`. `HeatModalCard` owns its own scrim (`Color.black.opacity(0.3)`, drag-to-dismiss, `.move(edge:.bottom)` transition collapsing under Reduce Motion) — discovered mid-build that `PlacesListOverlay`/`PassportSurface` (both built by concurrent sessions) already cite `HeatModalCard` by name as "the pattern they mirror," so this build matched their exact construction instead of inventing a third near-duplicate scrim mechanism.
  - **C6/C7** — `Passenger/Map/HeatComposition.swift` (pure `[Hood]`×hour→`[HoodFill]`) + `MapScreen`'s new `hoodFills` computed property, replacing the old per-Hood inline `densityStore.band(...)` call in the `Map`'s `ForEach`. `HeatRepaintSignpost.begin()` fires on a real hour change from either writer (`HourSlider`'s custom `Binding` setter, `EdgeHourZone`'s drag handler); `endIfPending()` fires immediately after `hoodFills` resolves.
  - **C8** — `DensityStore.refreshIfHourRolled()` gained two new call sites (`MapScreen.handleHeatButtonTap` on modal open, `EdgeHourZone.onTouchDown` on edge touch-down) plus the mid-`await` guard (capture `selectedHour` before `await load()`, skip the remap if it changed during the await). Guard covered by a test that smuggles a concurrent-edit simulation through the injected `now()` closure's second invocation — deterministic, no real concurrency needed.
  - **C9** — four new colour sets (`PillSurface`/`SliderFill`/`NowTick`/`EdgeRail`; `MutedOnSurface` already existed) — hex values computed and verified against the real WCAG formula before authoring, then re-verified by `HeatModalContrastTests` reading the actual compiled asset catalog (not the computed values assumed correct).
  - **C10** — `Passenger/Density/DensitySeed.swift` + `Resources/density-seed-tel-aviv.json` (5 real Hoods — florentin/kerem-hateimanim/neve-tzedek/lev-hair/old-north — satisfying the authoring rule: 4-6 transitions each for 3+ Hoods, 2 null hours on old-north) + `DensityStore.load()`'s new unconditional `BuildPhase.seedIsAuthoritative` branch, mirroring `PlaceCatalog.load()` exactly per the TRD.
  - **C11-C14** — `Passenger/EdgeHour/{EdgeGeometry,EdgeAvailability,EdgeHint,EdgeHourZone,EdgeHourTrack}.swift`. `EdgeGeometry`/`EdgeAvailability` are pure with full unit-test matrices (no simulator). `EdgeHourZone`'s `DragGesture(minimumDistance: 4)` latches vertical-dominance once via `@GestureState`, in the default `.local` coordinate space (v3's own correction); it only exists in `MapScreen`'s hierarchy when `EdgeAvailability.liveEdges(...)` includes that edge — structural removal, not an `isEnabled` flag, per D7 rule (c).
- **Disclosed deviation, not silent:** C10's literal "mirror `PlaceCatalog.load()` exactly" instruction makes `BuildPhase.seedIsAuthoritative`'s unconditional short-circuit fire on every `DensityStore.load()` call, with no per-instance override — identical to `PlaceCatalog`'s own check. This makes three of T-031's original `DensityStoreTests` (`liveLoadResolvesBand`, `failedFetchNoCacheIsUnavailable`, `failedFetchWithCacheFallsBack`, all built around injecting a `FakeDensityAPI`) unreachable through `load()` regardless of what the fakes are told to do — the TRD's own §4.5 preamble asks that these "pass unmodified alongside the new cases," which cannot hold in the literal sense once C10 is built as specified. `PlaceCatalogTests.swift` already carries this exact resolution for the identical shape of problem (its own header: "the live/cache branches are built to spec... but deliberately not exercised here"). Replaced the three with `seedIsAuthoritativeAfterLoad`/`loadMakesZeroFetchAttemptsWhileSeedIsAuthoritative`, direct precedents of `PlaceCatalogTests`' own `sourceIsSeedAfterLoad`/`loadMakesZeroFetchAttempts`. No unique coverage lost — `DensitySnapshot(rows:)`'s parsing/boundary-validation logic (what those three tests actually exercised) is independently and fully covered in `DensitySnapshotTests.swift`. Documented in the test file's own header for whoever reviews next, not left for a diff to explain on its own.
- **The TRD's own blocking condition, verified as a real test, not asserted by construction:** §9 row 7(e)/C13 says the step "is not done until" an in-band vertical edge drag is shown to move `selectedHour` while leaving `camera`/`MKCoordinateRegion` byte-identical — D7's whole hit-test argument is a prediction the TRD itself says needs confirming, not trusting. Neither value is otherwise observable from an XCUITest process, so `MapScreen.swift` gained two invisible, debug-only accessibility elements (`cameraRegionDebugDump`, `selectedHourDebugDump`) gated behind a new `-uiTestExposeCameraRegion` launch argument (same class of test-only seam as the pre-existing `-uiTestZoomedIn`) — absent from every real launch. `PassengerUITests/EdgeHourZoneInteractionTests.testInBandVerticalDragMovesTheHourAndLeavesTheCameraUntouched` reads both before/mid/after a real, synthesized in-band drag on a running app and asserts byte-identical camera + moved hour. **This test passed** — D7's hit-test prediction holds on this device/OS build (iPhone 17 Pro, iOS 26.5, simulator). Flagged for `ios-code-reviewer`/`qa` to re-run on an actual device before this is trusted as the last word, exactly as the TRD's own risk row asks.
- **Evidence:** full (not scoped) `xcodebuild -project Passenger.xcodeproj -scheme Passenger -destination 'platform=iOS Simulator,name=iPhone 17 Pro' build` — BUILD SUCCEEDED. Full `xcodebuild ... test` (not scoped) — **412/412 passed, zero failures**, up from the prior 400/400 (12 new: `HeatWiringTests` ×4, `HeatCompositionTests.resolvingAllHoodsIsFast` ×1 unit; `HeatButtonInteractionTests` ×3, `EdgeHourZoneInteractionTests` ×3, `HeatRepaintPerformanceTests` ×1 UI, plus dozens more across `DensitySeedTests`/`EdgeGeometryTests`/`EdgeAvailabilityTests`/`HourFormatTests`/`HeatModalContrastTests` folded into the same 412). The previously-flaky `SearchAccessibilityTests.testResultRowGrowsRatherThanTruncatesAtLargestAccessibilitySize` failed on the first full run today (unrelated to this task, a pre-existing search-feature Dynamic Type row-growth assertion) and passed clean on the second full run after this build's remaining pieces landed — consistent with this codebase's own well-documented simulator-flake class, not a regression this task introduced (confirmed by re-running the full suite twice, both times isolating this as the only failure and only on the first run).
- **Left behind:** (1) known minor gap, not a P0: `EdgeHint`'s show/hide transition (hint↔track swap) has no explicit `.transition`/`withAnimation` wrapping, so it doesn't literally "fade" under Reduce Motion the way §4.11's prose describes — structurally correct (never both visible at once), cosmetically unanimated. Cheap fix, not blocking. (2) The camera-immobility UI test result is simulator-only in this sandboxed session, same standing caveat `ColdOpenPerformanceTests`' own header already states for its metric — `qa`/a physical device should re-confirm before this is treated as fully closed, per the TRD's own risk-row language. (3) Ready for `ios-code-reviewer` — both signoffs (`ios-developer`+`ios-code-reviewer`) are the only ones `trd-review` named as owed, and this build note is the "what changed + how to test" handoff.
- **Git:** `passenger-code 198b082` — 33 files (18 new, 5 modified: `DensityStore.swift`, `MapNavRow.swift`, `MapScreen.swift`, `SettingsHint.swift` (not mine, kept as found), `DensityStoreTests.swift`). `passenger-brain` — `agent-os/BOARD.md` (both T-032 rows), `agent-os/PROGRESS.md` (this entry). Explicit paths, no `-A`. Re-read `git diff --staged` on both `BOARD.md`/`PROGRESS.md` before committing — no other session's hunks present in either at commit time.
